+    let rejectOld!: (error: Error) => void;
+    let completeNew!: () => void;
+    const downloadRenderResult = vi.fn(() => new Promise<void>((_resolve, reject) => { rejectOld = reject; }));
+    const nextDownload = vi.fn(() => new Promise<void>((resolve) => { completeNew = resolve; }));
+    mocks.useTimelineChromeContext.mockReturnValue(chrome({
+      downloadRenderResult, renderResultInfo: { revision: 7, completedAt: 1000 },
+    }));
+    const { rerender } = render(<TimelineRenderControls previewActionButtonClass="" />);
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Play last render/ }));
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Download' }));
+    expect(downloadRenderResult).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+
+    mocks.useTimelineChromeContext.mockReturnValue(chrome({
+      downloadRenderResult: nextDownload,
+      renderResultUrl: '/api/astrid/v1/objects/next.mp4',
+      renderResultInfo: { revision: 8, completedAt: 2000 },
+    }));
+    rerender(<TimelineRenderControls previewActionButtonClass="" />);
+    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Download' }));
+    expect(nextDownload).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
+
+    await act(async () => { rejectOld(new Error('The old render result changed.')); });
+    expect(screen.queryByRole('alert')).toBeNull();
+    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Downloading…' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
+    await act(async () => { completeNew(); });
+    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Download' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'false');
+  });

                     pass
 
+    def retained_incarnation_exited(self, pid: int, birth: str) -> bool:
+        """Return whether this exact retained incarnation has exited."""
+        with self._lock:
+            entry = self._handles.get(pid)
+            if entry is None or entry[0] != birth:
+                return False
+            handle = entry[1]
+            if handle is _PIDFD_RELEASED:
+                return True
+            if type(handle) is not int:
+                return False
+            poller = select.poll()
+            poller.register(handle, select.POLLIN)
+            return bool(poller.poll(0))
+

         with self._lock:
             if self._uncertain is not None:

+def _retained_incarnation_exited(process: subprocess.Popen, pid: int, birth: str) -> bool:
+    custody = getattr(process, "_astrid_custody", None)
+    proof = getattr(custody, "retained_incarnation_exited", None)
+    return bool(proof(pid, birth)) if callable(proof) else False

+def test_reaped_root_pid_reuse_does_not_poison_final_tree_observation(monkeypatch):
+    """A reaped root replacement is unrelated; known descendants remain fenced."""
+    process = SimpleNamespace(
+        pid=100,
+        returncode=0,
+        _astrid_process_birth="root",
+        _astrid_tree_members={100: "root", 200: "child"},
+        _astrid_custody=FakeCustody(),
+    )
+    monkeypatch.setattr(groups, "_process_snapshot", lambda: {
+        100: groups._ProcessInfo(100, 1, 100, "replacement-root"),
+        200: groups._ProcessInfo(200, 1, 200, "child"),
+        900: groups._ProcessInfo(900, 1, 900, "unrelated"),
+    })
+
+    assert groups.observe_tree(process) == {200: "child"}
+    assert not hasattr(process, "_astrid_tree_uncertain")
+    process._astrid_custody.retained_incarnation_exited = (
+        lambda pid, birth: pid == 200 and birth == "child"
+    )
+    monkeypatch.setattr(groups, "_process_snapshot", lambda: {
+        100: groups._ProcessInfo(100, 1, 100, "replacement-root"),
+        200: groups._ProcessInfo(200, 1, 200, "replacement-child"),
+    })
+    assert groups.observe_tree(process) == {}
+    assert not hasattr(process, "_astrid_tree_uncertain")
+    process._astrid_custody.retained_incarnation_exited = lambda *_: False
+    with pytest.raises(groups.CleanupUncertainError, match="owned process identity was reused"):
+        groups.observe_tree(process)
